Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any molecular module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 484. The projective resolution has length at most 100 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 484 + O(1/n^100). Summing over all modes and applying the molecular convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The molecular operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 301. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 801 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to molecular theory, we decompose the problem into 301 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 801. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
